inferred: []

- Source audit: current `RosBrokerDriver.reference_state` calls each `/query_state` service for an accepted moving interval. EXP-053 proves native `Trajectory.sample` has a mutable monotonic segment cursor under out-of-order queries. Direct arm/gripper goals retain the exact submitted trajectory and acceptance clock; MoveIt `ExecuteTrajectory` does not prove its downstream controller goal timing and must fail closed until separately validated. Published desired remains the authoritative label source at actual tick time. `REFERENCE_QUERY_INVALID`, `REFERENCE_INTERVAL_INVALID`, `PATH_TIMING_INVALID`, and stop/permit/deadline gates remain unchanged in meaning.

### EXP-054 source result (live validation pending)

- RED `scratch/20260924T034559Z-exp054-red-10079a97` proved a moving accepted direct goal invoked the mutable query service. A second RED `scratch/20260924T034721Z-exp054-publication-red-1abd7915` proved disagreement with published desired was not rejected. A third RED `scratch/20260924T034908Z-exp054-handle-red-6b3f945a` proved deep-copying a live action handle failed. All are intended code-boundary failures; scratch and stdout/JUnit retained.
- Source commit `f0fc2de6` reconstructs only one accepted direct `FollowJointTrajectory` interval from its exact submitted, zero-anchored, absolute-stamped trajectory using the separately native-checked Jazzy interpolator. It does not invoke `QueryTrajectoryState`. Published desired within the same interval must agree in position and velocity to absolute tolerance `1e-4`; mismatch returns `REFERENCE_QUERY_INVALID`. Missing, pending, canceled, ambiguous, MoveIt-downstream or malformed intervals fail closed. Goal state is snapshotted without copying the live action handle and rechecked after reconstruction. Ruling: a direct action acceptance plus an exact absolute stamped controller trajectory is sufficient for offline reconstruction of that direct controller goal; a MoveIt `ExecuteTrajectory` acceptance is not sufficient to prove downstream controller timing. Cost if wrong: live published-reference comparison must reject qualification before data can be accepted.
- Targeted reference matrix `scratch/20260924T035111Z-exp054-matrix-d98a4ebb`: 18 pass. Full ordinary `test/` xdist `scratch/20260924T035117Z-exp054-full-xdist-final-5bee6a9f`: 4163 collected, 3996 pass, 167 skip, 0 fail/error, 8 workers, exit 0, 26.29 s. Published live desired comparison, downstream MoveIt source, cancellation/Stop and physical motion remain unverified. EXP-054 is `RUNNING`, not accepted as a runtime result.
